  "proof-inputs/compare-surveys.py": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Compare retained, noncertifying all-rule surveys from one installed successor.\"\"\"\nimport argparse\nimport difflib\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport re\nimport sys\n\nLABELS = ('base', 'reference', 'reference-fault', 'estimate', 'estimate-fault')\nRULES = ['F1', 'F7', 'INV-CLASSIFY', 'K2', 'K3', 'K4', 'K5', 'K6',\n         'MOD-DIRECT-ENTRIES', 'MOD-INDEX', 'MOD-LAYOUT', 'PERF-THRESHOLD',\n         'REL-EXTRACT', 'SPEC-SCENARIOS']\nSELECTED = {\n    'reference': 'apps/wbs/fe-01/src/components/wbs/reference-set-field.tsx',\n    'estimate': 'apps/wbs/fe-01/src/components/wbs/plan-columns/estimates.tsx',\n}\nENVELOPE = {'schemaVersion', 'candidate', 'policy', 'allowed', 'ruleIds',\n            'findings', 'unevaluated', 'certifies'}\nSNAPSHOT = {'manifest', 'executableSha256', 'descriptor', 'descriptorSha256',\n            'validatorSha256', 'bunVersion'}\n\n\nclass Refusal(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\ndef require(condition, message):\n    if not condition:\n        raise Refusal(message)\n\n\ndef equal(actual, expected, label):\n    if actual != expected:\n        before = json.dumps(expected, indent=2, sort_keys=True).splitlines()\n        after = json.dumps(actual, indent=2, sort_keys=True).splitlines()\n        difference = '\\n'.join(difflib.unified_diff(\n            before, after, fromfile='expected', tofile='observed', lineterm=''))\n        raise Refusal(f'{label}\\n{difference}')\n\n\ndef unique_object(pairs):\n    record = {}\n    for key, value in pairs:\n        require(key not in record, f'duplicate JSON key: {key}')\n        record[key] = value\n    return record\n\n\ndef refuse_constant(value):\n    raise Refusal(f'nonfinite JSON constant: {value}')\n\n\ndef read_json(path):\n    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(), object_pairs_hook=unique_object,\n                      parse_constant=refuse_constant)\n\n\ndef digest(path):\n    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef text(value):\n    return isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip())\n\n\ndef sha256(value):\n    return isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', value) is not None\n\n\ndef resolved(path, cwd):\n    return (Path(cwd) / path).resolve(strict=True)\n\n\ndef validate_snapshot(snapshot, label):\n    require(isinstance(snapshot, dict), f'{label}: snapshot must be an object')\n    equal(sorted(snapshot), sorted(SNAPSHOT), f'{label}: snapshot fields')\n    for key in ('executableSha256', 'descriptorSha256', 'validatorSha256'):\n        require(sha256(snapshot[key]), f'{label}: malformed {key}')\n f'{label}: malformed finding: {finding}')\n        require('subject' not in finding or text(finding['subject']),\n                f'{label}: malformed subject: {finding}')\n        require(finding['effect'] == 'debt', f'{label}: non-debt finding under all-observe policy')\n    unjudged = []\n    for entry in report['unevaluated']:\n        require(isinstance(entry, dict) and invalid unevaluated rule/reason: {entry}')\n        unjudged.append(entry['ruleId'])\n    require(len(set(unjudged)) == len(unjudged), f'{label}: duplicate unevaluated rule')\n    require(not stream:\n        stream.write(json.dumps(value, indent=2) + '\\n')\n\n\ndef extract_successor(archive, destination):\n    # Proof: tiny ZIP byte/size/member/package mutations fail this exact digest')\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as packed:\n        members = [member for member in packed.infolist() if member.filename == CONFIG['artifactMember']]\n        survey.require(len(members) == 1 and not members[0].is_dir(), 'successor member absent or ambiguous')\n        content = packed.read(members[0])\n    survey.equal(sha(content), CONFIG['successorArchiveSha256'], 'successor repository)\n        survey.equal(tree, binding['tree'], label + ': candidate tree')\n        survey.equal(binding['identity']['selection'],\n                     {'kind': 'committed', 'revision': binding['commit'], 'tree': tree},\n                     label + ': candidate selection')\n    for label, path in survey.SELECTED.items():\n        fault = bindings['bindings'][label + fault parent')\n        survey.equal(support.query(['git', 'diff', '--name-only', good, fault], repository), path,\n                     label + ': fault changed unrelated path')\n        survey.equal(support.query(['git', 'rev-parse', fault + ':' + path], repository),\n                     support.query(['git', 'rev-parse', CONFIG['faultPrerequisite'] + ':' + path], repository),\n                     label + ': fault did not restore original blob')\n\n\ndef validate_helpers():\n    # Proof: changing any copied helper or guard byte refuses before command launch.\n    for name, digest')\n\n\ndef run_survey(output, prefix, command, consumer):\n    validate_helpers()\n    # Proof: an existing report/receipt/snapshot prefix is rejected by prove-adapter.py.\n    for suffix in ('.stdout', '.stderr', '.receipt.json', '.guard.stdout', '.guard.stderr', '.guard-exit.json'):\n        survey.require(not (output / (prefix + suffix)).exists(), 'stale survey output: ' + prefix + suffix)\n    guard = ROOT / CONFIG['guards']['900']['path']\n    invocation = ['python3', str(guard), str(output / prefix), *command]\n    run = subprocess.run(invocation, cwd=consumer, 0, 'survey guard process failed')\n    receipt = survey.read_json(output / (prefix + '.receipt.json'))\n    report = survey.read_json(output / (prefix + '.stdout'))\n    survey.require(type(report.get('allowed')) is bool, 'report allowed must be boolean')\n    acceptance.validate_guard(receipt, command, consumer, 0 if report['allowed'] else 1)\n    return report\n\n\ndef validate_baseline(report):\n    # Proof: compiler refusal and changed compiler finding fail the exact baseline comparison.\n    expected = survey.read_json(ROOT / 'inputs/expected-base.json.txt')\n    survey.equal(report, expected, 'hosted baseline differs from published final-source baseline')\n\n\ndef snapshot(output, name, consumer, expected):\n    identity                validate_baseline(report)\n            finally:\n                after = snapshot(output, prefix + '.after', consumer, expected_identity)\n                support.require_same_identity(before, after, prefix)\n        if mode == 'full':\n            command = ['python3', str(ROOT / 'compare-surveys.py'), '--bindings', str(ROOT /
